// ---- posix-cmds.js ---- /* TerminalEX — POSIX commands: shell builtins, files, and text processing */ (function () { 'use strict'; const TX = window.TX; const C = TX.C; const FS_MSG = TX.FS_MSG; const cmds = (TX.posixCmds = TX.posixCmds || {}); const def = (names, fn, opts = {}) => names.split(' ').forEach((n) => { cmds[n] = Object.assign({ fn }, opts); }); TX.defPosix = def; const getopt = (...a) => TX.getopt(...a); const lines = TX.splitLines; // ------------------------------------------------------------------ helpers function permStr(node) { const t = node.t === 'd' ? 'd' : node.t === 'l' ? 'l' : '-'; const m = node.mode; let s = ''; for (const sh of [6, 3, 0]) s += ((m >> sh) & 4 ? 'r' : '-') + ((m >> sh) & 2 ? 'w' : '-') + ((m >> sh) & 1 ? 'x' : '-'); return t + s; } TX.permStr = permStr; function isExec(node, sh) { if (node.t !== 'f') return false; if (sh && sh.flavor === 'gitbash') return /\.(exe|sh|bat|cmd|com)$/i.test(node.name); return !!(node.mode & 0o111); } function colorName(node, name, sh, target) { const n = target || node; if (node.t === 'l') return C.style(['bold', 'cyan'], name); if (n.t === 'd') return (n.mode & 0o002) ? '\x1b[30;42m' + name + '\x1b[0m' : C.style(['bold', 'blue'], name); if (isExec(n, sh)) return C.style(['bold', 'green'], name); if (/\.(tar|tgz|gz|zip|bz2|xz|7z|deb|rpm|iso|dmg|rar)$/i.test(name)) return C.style(['bold', 'red'], name); if (/\.(png|jpe?g|gif|bmp|svg|webp|mp4|mkv|mov|avi)$/i.test(name)) return C.style(['bold', 'magenta'], name); if (/\.(mp3|wav|flac|m4a|ogg|m3u)$/i.test(name)) return C.cyan(name); return name; } TX.colorName = colorName; function owner(sh, node) { if (sh.flavor === 'gitbash') return ['student', '197121']; if (sh.m.kind === 'mac') return [node.uid === 'root' ? 'root' : node.uid, node.uid === 'root' ? 'wheel' : (node.gid === node.uid ? 'staff' : node.gid)]; return [node.uid, node.gid]; } function nlinks(node) { if (node.t !== 'd') return 1; return 2 + Object.values(node.ch).filter((c) => c.t === 'd').length; } function fsErr(c, verb, p, e) { if (!(e instanceof TX.FsError)) throw e; if (c.sh.flavor === 'zsh' && c.m.kind === 'mac') return c.err(`${c.name}: ${p}: ${FS_MSG[e.code]}\n`); c.err(`${c.name}: ${verb ? verb + ' ' : ''}'${p}': ${FS_MSG[e.code] || e.message}\n`); } TX.fsErr = fsErr; function canEnter(sh, node, user) { if (user === 'root' || !node) return true; if (node.uid === user) return true; if (sh.flavor === 'gitbash') return true; return !!(node.mode & 0o001) || (node.gid === user && (node.mode & 0o010)); } TX.canEnter = canEnter; // BRE -> JS regex source function bre(src, extended) { if (extended) return src; let out = ''; for (let i = 0; i < src.length; i++) { const ch = src[i]; if (ch === '\\' && i + 1 < src.length) { const n = src[i + 1]; if ('(){}|+?'.includes(n)) { out += n; i++; continue; } out += ch + n; i++; continue; } if ('(){}|+?'.includes(ch)) { out += '\\' + ch; continue; } out += ch; } return out; } function posixClasses(s) { return s.replace(/\[:alpha:\]/g, 'a-zA-Z').replace(/\[:digit:\]/g, '0-9').replace(/\[:alnum:\]/g, 'a-zA-Z0-9').replace(/\[:upper:\]/g, 'A-Z').replace(/\[:lower:\]/g, 'a-z').replace(/\[:space:\]/g, '\\s').replace(/\[:punct:\]/g, '!-\\/:-@\\[-`{-~'); } TX.bre = (s, e) => posixClasses(bre(s, e)); // ------------------------------------------------------------------ builtins def('cd', (c) => { const sh = c.sh; let target = c.args.filter((a) => a !== '-P' && a !== '-L')[0]; if (c.args.length > 2 || (c.args.length === 2 && !c.args[0].startsWith('-'))) return c.fail('too many arguments'); if (target === undefined || target === '') target = sh.fmt(sh.home); if (target === '-') { if (!sh.vars.OLDPWD) return c.fail('OLDPWD not set'); target = sh.vars.OLDPWD; c.outln(target); } const segs = sh.resolve(target); const node = sh.vfs.get(segs); const zsh = sh.flavor === 'zsh'; if (!node) { c.err(zsh ? `cd: no such file or directory: ${target}\n` : `${c.sh.name}: cd: ${target}: No such file or directory\n`); return 1; } if (node.t !== 'd') { c.err(zsh ? `cd: not a directory: ${target}\n` : `${c.sh.name}: cd: ${target}: Not a directory\n`); return 1; } if (!canEnter(sh, node, c.user)) { c.err(zsh ? `cd: permission denied: ${target}\n` : `${c.sh.name}: cd: ${target}: Permission denied\n`); return 1; } sh.vars.OLDPWD = sh.fmt(sh.cwd); sh.cwd = segs; sh.vars.PWD = sh.fmt(segs); return 0; }); def('pwd', (c) => { c.outln(c.sh.fmt(c.sh.cwd)); }); function escapes(s) { return s.replace(/\\(0[0-7]{0,3}|x[0-9a-fA-F]{1,2}|u[0-9a-fA-F]{4}|e|[abfnrtv\\c])/g, (all, k) => { switch (k[0]) { case 'n': return '\n'; case 't': return '\t'; case 'r': return '\r'; case 'a': return '\x07'; case 'b': return '\b'; case 'f': return '\f'; case 'v': return '\v'; case '\\': return '\\'; case 'e': return '\x1b'; case 'c': return '\u0000STOP'; case '0': return String.fromCharCode(parseInt(k.slice(1) || '0', 8)); case 'x': return String.fromCharCode(parseInt(k.slice(1), 16)); case 'u': return String.fromCharCode(parseInt(k.slice(1), 16)); } return all; }); } TX.escapes = escapes; def('echo', (c) => { let a = c.args.slice(), nl = true, esc = c.sh.flavor === 'zsh'; while (a.length && /^-[neE]+$/.test(a[0])) { const f = a.shift(); if (f.includes('n')) nl = false; if (f.includes('e')) esc = true; if (f.includes('E')) esc = false; } let s = a.join(' '); if (esc) { s = escapes(s); const k = s.indexOf('\u0000STOP'); if (k >= 0) { s = s.slice(0, k); nl = false; } } c.out(s + (nl ? '\n' : '')); }); function sprintf(fmt, args) { let out = '', ai = 0, used = false; const run = () => { used = false; out += escapes(fmt).replace(/%([-+ 0#]*)(\d+|\*)?(?:\.(\d+))?([sdifxXoceEgGb%])/g, (all, fl, w, p, t) => { if (t === '%') return '%'; used = true; let v = args[ai++]; if (v === undefined) v = t === 's' || t === 'b' ? '' : '0'; let s; switch (t) { case 'd': case 'i': s = String(Math.trunc(Number(v) || 0)); break; case 'f': s = (Number(v) || 0).toFixed(p === undefined ? 6 : +p); break; case 'e': case 'E': s = (Number(v) || 0).toExponential(p === undefined ? 6 : +p); if (t === 'E') s = s.toUpperCase(); break; case 'g': case 'G': s = String(Number(v) || 0); break; case 'x': s = (Math.trunc(Number(v)) >>> 0).toString(16); break; case 'X': s = (Math.trunc(Number(v)) >>> 0).toString(16).toUpperCase(); break; case 'o': s = (Math.trunc(Number(v)) >>> 0).toString(8); break; case 'c': s = String(v)[0] || ''; break; case 'b': s = escapes(String(v)); break; default: s = String(v); if (p !== undefined) s = s.slice(0, +p); } if (w) { const n = +w; if (fl.includes('-')) s = s.padEnd(n); else if (fl.includes('0') && t !== 's') s = s.padStart(n, '0'); else s = s.padStart(n); } return s; }); }; run(); while (used && ai < args.length) run(); return out; } TX.sprintf = sprintf; def('printf', (c) => { if (!c.args.length) return c.fail('usage: printf [-v var] format [arguments]', 2); c.out(sprintf(c.args[0], c.args.slice(1))); }); def('export declare typeset', (c) => { const sh = c.sh; const args = c.args.filter((a) => !/^-[a-zA-Z]+$/.test(a)); if (!args.length) { Object.keys(sh.vars).sort().forEach((k) => { if (c.name !== 'export' || sh.exported.has(k)) c.outln(`declare -x ${k}="${sh.vars[k]}"`); }); return 0; } for (const a of args) { const m = /^([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)(?:=(.*))?$/s.exec(a); if (!m) { c.err(`${c.sh.name}: export: \`${a}': not a valid identifier\n`); return 1; } if (m[2] !== undefined) sh.vars[m[1]] = m[2]; else if (sh.vars[m[1]] === undefined) sh.vars[m[1]] = ''; if (c.name === 'export') sh.exported.add(m[1]); } }); def('local readonly', (c) => { c.args.forEach((a) => { const m = /^([A-Za-z_]\w*)(?:=(.*))?$/s.exec(a); if (m) c.sh.vars[m[1]] = m[2] || ''; }); }); def('unset', (c) => { c.args.filter((a) => !a.startsWith('-')).forEach((a) => { delete c.sh.vars[a]; delete c.sh.funcs[a]; c.sh.exported.delete(a); }); }); def('set', (c) => { if (!c.args.length) { Object.keys(c.sh.vars).sort().forEach((k) => c.outln(`${k}='${c.sh.vars[k]}'`)); return; } if (c.args[0] === '--') { c.sh.params = c.args.slice(1); return; } if (!c.args[0].startsWith('-') && !c.args[0].startsWith('+')) c.sh.params = c.args.slice(); }); def('env printenv', (c) => { const sh = c.sh; if (c.name === 'printenv' && c.args.length) { let code = 1; c.args.forEach((k) => { if (sh.vars[k] !== undefined) { c.outln(sh.vars[k]); code = 0; } }); return code; } const assigns = [], rest = []; for (const a of c.args) { if (!rest.length && /^[A-Za-z_]\w*=/.test(a)) assigns.push(a); else rest.push(a); } if (rest.length && c.name === 'env') { const saved = {}; assigns.forEach((a) => { const [k, v] = a.split(/=(.*)/s); saved[k] = sh.vars[k]; sh.vars[k] = v; }); return sh.invoke(rest, c.io).finally(() => Object.keys(saved).forEach((k) => { if (saved[k] === undefined) delete sh.vars[k]; else sh.vars[k] = saved[k]; })); } [...sh.exported].sort().forEach((k) => { if (sh.vars[k] !== undefined) c.outln(k + '=' + sh.vars[k]); }); assigns.forEach((a) => c.outln(a)); }); def('alias', (c) => { const sh = c.sh; if (!c.args.length || c.args[0] === '-p') { Object.keys(sh.aliases).sort().forEach((k) => c.outln(`alias ${k}='${sh.aliases[k]}'`)); return; } let code = 0; for (const a of c.args) { const eq = a.indexOf('='); if (eq < 0) { if (sh.aliases[a] !== undefined) c.outln(`alias ${a}='${sh.aliases[a]}'`); else { c.err(`${c.sh.name}: alias: ${a}: not found\n`); code = 1; } continue; } sh.aliases[a.slice(0, eq)] = a.slice(eq + 1); } return code; }); def('unalias', (c) => { if (c.args[0] === '-a') { c.sh.aliases = {}; return; } c.args.forEach((a) => { if (c.sh.aliases[a] === undefined) c.err(`${c.sh.name}: unalias: ${a}: not found\n`); delete c.sh.aliases[a]; }); }); def('history', (c) => { const H = c.sh.history; if (c.args[0] === '-c') { H.length = 0; return; } let n = parseInt(c.args[0], 10); const start = isNaN(n) ? 0 : Math.max(0, H.length - n); for (let i = start; i < H.length; i++) c.outln(String(i + 1).padStart(5) + ' ' + H[i]); }); def('exit logout', (c) => { throw new TX.ExitShell(parseInt(c.args[0], 10) || 0); }); def('source .', async (c) => { if (!c.args.length) return c.fail('filename argument required', 2); let text; try { text = c.vfs.readFile(c.path(c.args[0]), { user: c.user }); } catch (e) { c.err(`${c.sh.name}: ${c.args[0]}: ${FS_MSG[e.code]}\n`); return 1; } const saved = c.sh.params; c.sh.params = c.args.slice(1); try { return await c.sh.run(text.replace(/^#!.*\n/, ''), c.io); } finally { c.sh.params = saved; } }); def('bash sh zsh', async (c) => { const sh = c.sh; const args = c.args.slice(); if (args[0] === '-c') return sh.run(args[1] || '', c.io); if (args[0] === '--version') { c.outln(c.name === 'zsh' ? 'zsh 5.9 (arm64-apple-darwin24.0)' : 'GNU bash, version 5.2.21(1)-release (x86_64-pc-linux-gnu)\nCopyright (C) 2022 Free Software Foundation, Inc.'); return; } if (args.length) { let text; try { text = c.vfs.readFile(c.path(args[0]), { user: c.user }); } catch (e) { c.err(`${c.name}: ${args[0]}: ${FS_MSG[e.code]}\n`); return 127; } return sh.runScript(text, args, c.io); } if (c.stdin != null) return sh.run(c.stdin, c.io); const flavor = c.name === 'zsh' ? 'zsh' : sh.flavor; sh.session.pushShell(new TX.PosixShell(sh.session, sh.m, flavor, { user: sh.user, cwd: sh.cwd })); }, { only: ['linux', 'mac', 'gitbash'] }); def('type', (c) => { let code = 0; for (const n of c.args) { if (c.sh.aliases[n] !== undefined) c.outln(`${n} is aliased to \`${c.sh.aliases[n]}'`); else if (c.sh.funcs[n]) c.outln(`${n} is a function`); else if (['cd', 'echo', 'export', 'alias', 'pwd', 'history', 'exit', 'source', 'type', 'set', 'unset', 'read', 'test', 'printf', 'kill', 'jobs', 'true', 'false'].includes(n)) c.outln(`${n} is a shell builtin`); else if (['if', 'then', 'else', 'fi', 'for', 'while', 'do', 'done'].includes(n)) c.outln(`${n} is a shell keyword`); else if (c.sh.available(n)) c.outln(`${n} is ${binPath(c.sh, n)}`); else { c.err(`${c.sh.name}: type: ${n}: not found\n`); code = 1; } } return code; }); function binPath(sh, n) { if (sh.flavor === 'gitbash') return '/usr/bin/' + n; if (sh.m.kind === 'mac') return (TX.posixCmds[n] && TX.posixCmds[n].pkg && !['git', 'curl', 'nano', 'vim', 'python3'].includes(TX.posixCmds[n].pkg) ? '/opt/homebrew/bin/' : '/usr/bin/') + n; return '/usr/bin/' + n; } TX.binPath = binPath; def('which', (c) => { let code = 0; for (const n of c.args.filter((a) => !a.startsWith('-'))) { if (c.sh.available(n)) c.outln(binPath(c.sh, n)); else { code = 1; if (c.sh.flavor === 'zsh') c.outln(n + ' not found'); else if (c.sh.flavor === 'gitbash') c.err(`which: no ${n} in (${c.sh.vars.PATH})\n`); } } return code; }); def('whereis', (c) => { c.args.forEach((n) => c.outln(n + ':' + (c.sh.available(n) ? ' ' + binPath(c.sh, n) + ' /usr/share/man/man1/' + n + '.1.gz' : ''))); }); def('command', (c) => { if (c.args[0] === '-v') { const n = c.args[1]; if (c.sh.lookup(n)) { c.outln(c.sh.aliases[n] ? `alias ${n}='${c.sh.aliases[n]}'` : binPath(c.sh, n)); return 0; } return 1; } return c.sh.invoke(c.args, c.io, { noFunc: true }); }); def('true :', () => 0); def('false', () => 1); def('return', (c) => { c.sh.returnFlag = true; c.sh.returnCode = parseInt(c.args[0], 10) || 0; return c.sh.returnCode; }); def('break continue', (c) => { if (c.name === 'break') c.sh.breakFlag = true; return 0; }); def('shift', (c) => { c.sh.params = c.sh.params.slice(parseInt(c.args[0], 10) || 1); }); def('eval', (c) => c.sh.run(c.args.join(' '), c.io)); def('let', (c) => { let v = 0; try { for (const a of c.args) v = TX.arith(a, (k) => c.sh.vars[k], (k, x) => { c.sh.vars[k] = x; }); } catch (e) { return c.fail(e.message); } return v ? 0 : 1; }); def('read', async (c) => { const { f, rest } = getopt(c.args, 'p:srn:t:a:d:'); let line; if (c.stdin != null) { const L = c.stdin.split('\n'); line = L.shift(); c.io.stdin = L.join('\n'); if (line === undefined || (line === '' && !L.length)) return 1; } else { if (f.p) c.term.write(f.p); line = await c.readLine('', { hidden: !!f.s }); if (f.s) c.term.write(''); } const names = rest.length ? rest : ['REPLY']; if (f.a) { c.sh.vars[f.a] = line; return 0; } const parts = line.trim().split(/\s+/); names.forEach((n, i) => { c.sh.vars[n] = i === names.length - 1 ? parts.slice(i).join(' ') : (parts[i] || ''); }); return 0; }); function test(args, c) { const sh = c.sh; const st = (p) => sh.vfs.get(sh.resolve(p)); const un = { '-e': (p) => !!st(p), '-f': (p) => { const n = st(p); return !!n && n.t === 'f'; }, '-d': (p) => { const n = st(p); return !!n && n.t === 'd'; }, '-s': (p) => { const n = st(p); return !!n && sh.vfs.size(n) > 0; }, '-r': (p) => !!st(p), '-w': (p) => { try { sh.vfs.checkWrite(sh.resolve(p), c.user); return !!st(p); } catch (e) { return false; } }, '-x': (p) => { const n = st(p); return !!n && (n.t === 'd' || isExec(n, sh)); }, '-L': (p) => { const n = sh.vfs.get(sh.resolve(p), false); return !!n && n.t === 'l'; }, '-h': (p) => { const n = sh.vfs.get(sh.resolve(p), false); return !!n && n.t === 'l'; }, '-z': (s) => s === '', '-n': (s) => s !== '' }; const num = (x) => { const n = parseInt(x, 10); if (isNaN(n)) throw new Error(`${x}: integer expression expected`); return n; }; const bin = { '=': (a, b) => a === b, '==': (a, b) => a === b, '!=': (a, b) => a !== b, '-eq': (a, b) => num(a) === num(b), '-ne': (a, b) => num(a) !== num(b), '-lt': (a, b) => num(a) < num(b), '-le': (a, b) => num(a) <= num(b), '-gt': (a, b) => num(a) > num(b), '-ge': (a, b) => num(a) >= num(b), '<': (a, b) => a < b, '>': (a, b) => a > b, '=~': (a, b) => new RegExp(b).test(a) }; function expr(a) { const orIdx = a.findIndex((x) => x === '-o' || x === '||'); if (orIdx > 0) return expr(a.slice(0, orIdx)) || expr(a.slice(orIdx + 1)); const andIdx = a.findIndex((x) => x === '-a' || x === '&&'); if (andIdx > 0) return expr(a.slice(0, andIdx)) && expr(a.slice(andIdx + 1)); if (a[0] === '!') return !expr(a.slice(1)); if (a[0] === '(' && a[a.length - 1] === ')') return expr(a.slice(1, -1)); if (a.length === 0) return false; if (a.length === 1) return a[0] !== ''; if (a.length === 2 && un[a[0]]) return un[a[0]](a[1]); if (a.length === 3 && bin[a[1]]) return bin[a[1]](a[0], a[2]); throw new Error(`${a[0]}: unary operator expected`); } return expr(args); } def('test [ [[', (c) => { let a = c.args.slice(); if (c.name === '[') { if (a[a.length - 1] !== ']') return c.fail("missing `]'", 2); a.pop(); } if (c.name === '[[') { if (a[a.length - 1] !== ']]') return c.fail("missing `]]'", 2); a.pop(); } try { return test(a, c) ? 0 : 1; } catch (e) { c.err(`${c.sh.name}: ${c.name}: ${e.message}\n`); return 2; } }); def('sleep', async (c) => { if (!c.args.length) return c.fail('missing operand'); let ms = 0; for (const a of c.args) { const m = /^(\d*\.?\d+)([smhd]?)$/.exec(a); if (!m) return c.fail(`invalid time interval '${a}'`); ms += parseFloat(m[1]) * ({ '': 1, s: 1, m: 60, h: 3600, d: 86400 }[m[2]]) * 1000; } await c.sleep(Math.min(ms, 1000 * 60 * 10)); }); def('time', async (c) => { const t0 = performance.now(); const code = c.args.length ? await c.sh.invoke(c.args, c.io) : 0; const s = (performance.now() - t0) / 1000; const f = (x) => Math.floor(x / 60) + 'm' + (x % 60).toFixed(3) + 's'; c.err(`\nreal\t${f(s)}\nuser\t${f(s * 0.08)}\nsys\t${f(s * 0.03)}\n`); return code; }); def('jobs', (c) => { c.sh.jobs.forEach((j) => c.outln(`[${j.id}]${j === c.sh.jobs[c.sh.jobs.length - 1] ? '+' : '-'} ${j.done ? 'Done' : 'Running'} ${j.text}${j.done ? '' : ' &'}`)); c.sh.jobs = c.sh.jobs.filter((j) => !j.done); }); def('fg bg wait', async (c) => { const j = c.sh.jobs[c.sh.jobs.length - 1]; if (!j) return c.fail('current: no such job'); if (c.name === 'bg') { c.outln(`[${j.id}]+ ${j.text} &`); return; } if (c.name === 'fg') c.outln(j.text); while (!j.done) await c.sleep(100); }); def('clear reset', (c) => { c.term.clear(); }); def('help', (c) => { c.out(TX.docs.posixHelp(c.sh)); }); def('man', async (c) => { const n = c.args.filter((a) => !/^\d$/.test(a))[0]; if (!n) { c.err('What manual page do you want?\nFor example, try \'man man\'.\n'); return 1; } const page = TX.docs.manPage(n, c.sh); if (!page) { c.err(`No manual entry for ${n}\n`); return 16; } if (!c.tty) { c.out(TX.stripAnsi(page)); return; } await TX.apps.pager(c.term, page, `Manual page ${n}(1) line %L (press h for help or q to quit)`); }); def('whatis', (c) => { c.args.forEach((n) => { const d = TX.docs.get(n); c.outln(d ? `${n} (1)${' '.repeat(Math.max(1, 14 - n.length))}- ${d.summary}` : `${n}: nothing appropriate.`); }); }); def('apropos', (c) => { const q = (c.args[0] || '').toLowerCase(); TX.docs.all().filter((d) => d.name.includes(q) || d.summary.toLowerCase().includes(q)).forEach((d) => c.outln(`${d.name} (1)${' '.repeat(Math.max(1, 14 - d.name.length))}- ${d.summary}`)); }); // ------------------------------------------------------------------ ls def('ls', (c) => { const sh = c.sh; const { f, rest } = getopt(c.args, 'laAhR1tSrdFGiCsnog', { all: 'a', 'almost-all': 'A', 'human-readable': 'h', recursive: 'R', reverse: 'r', directory: 'd', classify: 'F', color: true, size: 's' }); const color = c.tty && f['--color'] !== 'never' && (f['--color'] || f.G || ['bash', 'zsh', 'gitbash'].includes(sh.flavor) || !!sh.vars.CLICOLOR); const long = f.l || f.n || f.o || f.g; const targets = rest.length ? rest : ['.']; let code = 0; const fileTargets = [], dirTargets = []; for (const t of targets) { const segs = c.path(t); const lnode = sh.vfs.get(segs, false); const node = sh.vfs.get(segs); if (!lnode) { c.err(sh.m.kind === 'mac' ? -1 : key(a) > key(b) ? 1 : a.name < b.name ? -1 : 1)); if (f.r) entries.reverse(); const deco = (e) => { const target = e.node.t === 'l' ? sh.vfs.get(e.node.tgt) : null; let s = color ? colorName(e.node, e.name, sh, target) : e.name; if (e.name.includes(' ') && sh.m.kind === 'linux' && c.tty) s = "'" + s + "'"; if (f.F) s += e.node.t === 'd' ? isLast : a.line ? a.line === li + 1 : a.re.test(line)); for (const cmd of cmdsList) { let sel = true; if (cmd.a1 && !cmd.a2) sel = matchAddr(cmd.a1); else if (cmd.a1 && cmd.a2) { const active = ranges.get(cmd); if (active) { sel = true; if (cmd.a2.line ? li + 1 >= cmd.a2.line : matchAddr(cmd.a2)) ranges.delete(cmd); } else if (matchAddr(cmd.a1)) { sel = true; if (!(cmd.a2.line && cmd.a2.line <= li + 1)) ranges.set(cmd, true); } else sel = false; } if (cmd.neg) sel = !sel; if (!sel) continue; switch (cmd.op) { case 's': { if (cmd.nth) { let k = 0; const re = new RegExp(cmd.re.source, cmd.re.flags.includes('g') ? cmd.to[k] : ch; }).join(''); break; case 'd': deleted = true; break; case 'p': out.push(line); break; case '=': out.push(String(li + 1)); break; case 'a': after.push(cmd.text); break; case 'i': out.push(cmd.text); break; case 'c': line = cmd.text; break; case 'G': line += '\n'; break; case 'q': quit = true; break; } if (deleted) break; } if (!deleted && !f.n) out.push(line); out.push(...after); } return out.length ? out.join('\n') + '\n' : ''; }; if (f.i) { for (const p of files) { try { const segs = c.path(p); c.vfs.writeFile(segs, process(c.vfs.readFile(segs, { user: c.user })), { user: c.user }); } catch (e) { c.err(`sed: can't read ${p}: ${FS_MSG[e.code]}\n`); return 2; } } return 0; } let code = 0; for (const inp of c.input(files)) { if (inp.text === null) { code = 2; continue; } c.out(process(inp.text)); } return code; }); def('awk gawk mawk', (c) => { const { f, rest } = getopt(c.args, 'F:v:f:'); let prog = rest.shift(); if (f.f) { try { prog = c.vfs.readFile(c.path(f.f)); rest.unshift(prog); prog = rest.shift(); } catch (e) { return c.fail(`fatal: can't open source file \`${f.f}'`, 2); } } if (prog === undefined) { c.err('usage: awk [-F fs][-v var=value][prog | -f progfile][file ...]\n'); return 2; } const vars = {}; if (f.v) { const [k, v] = String(f.v).split('='); vars[k] = v; } try { const inputs = c.input(rest); const out = TX.awk(prog, inputs.filter((x) => x.text !== null), { FS: f.F !== undefined ? '^' + pat + '$' : pat); const hits = c.m.procs.filter((p) => re.test(f.f ? p.cmd : p.name)); if (c.name === 'pidof') { if (hits.length) c.outln(hits.map((p) => p.pid).reverse().join(' ')); return hits.length ? 0 : 1; } hits.forEach((p) => c.outln(f.l || f.a ? p.pid + ' ' + (f.a ? p.cmd : p.name) : String(p.pid))); return hits.length ? 0 : 1; }); function killPid(c, pid, sig) { const p = c.m.procs.find((x) => x.pid === pid); if (!p) { c.err(`${c.sh.flavor === 'zsh' ? 'kill: kill ' + pid + ' failed: no such process' : `${c.sh.name}: kill: (${pid}) - No such process`}\n`); return 1; } if (p.user !== c.user && c.user !== 'root') { c.err(c.sh.flavor === 'zsh' ? `kill: kill ${pid} failed: operation not permitted\n` : `${c.sh.name}: kill: (${pid}) - Operation not permitted\n`); return 1; } if (pid === 1) { c.err(c.sh.name + ': kill: (1) - Operation not permitted\n'); return 1; } if (sig === 'STOP' || sig === '19') { p.stat = 'T'; return 0; } if (sig === 'CONT' || sig === '18') { p.stat = 'S'; return 0; } c.m.kill(pid); const svc = c.m.services.find((s) => s.pid === pid); if (svc) svc.active = false; return 0; } def('kill', (c) => { if (c.args[0] === '-l' || c.args[0] === '-L') { c.outln(' 1) SIGHUP\t 2) SIGINT\t 3) SIGQUIT\t 4) SIGILL\t 5) SIGTRAP\n 6) SIGABRT\t 7) SIGBUS\t 8) SIGFPE\t 9) SIGKILL\t10) SIGUSR1\n11) SIGSEGV\t12) SIGUSR2\t13) SIGPIPE\t14) SIGALRM\t15) SIGTERM\n17) SIGCHLD\t18) SIGCONT\t19) SIGSTOP\t20) SIGTSTP\t21) SIGTTIN'); return; } let sig = 'TERM'; const pids = []; for (const a of c.args) { if (/^-(\d+|[A-Z]+)$/.test(a)) sig = a.slice(1).replace(/^SIG/, ''); else if (a === '-s') continue; else if (/^%\d+$/.test(a)) { const j = c.sh.jobs.find((x) => x.id === +a.slice(1)); if (j) { pids.push(j.pid); j.done = true; j.code = 143; } else c.err(`${c.sh.name}: kill: ${a}: no such job\n`); } else if (/^\d+$/.test(a)) pids.push(+a); else { c.err(`${c.sh.name}: kill: ${a}: arguments must be process or job IDs\n`); return 1; } } if (!pids.length) { c.err('kill: usage: kill [-s sigspec | -n signum | -sigspec] pid | jobspec ... or kill -l [sigspec]\n'); return 2; } let code = 0; pids.forEach((p) => { code |= killPid(c, p, sig); }); return code; }); def('killall pkill', (c) => { const { rest } = getopt(c.args, 'fiu:9s:'); const pat = rest[0]; if (!pat) return c.fail('no process name specified'); const re = c.name === 'killall' ? C.style(['bold', 'green'], '●') : '○'; const lines = [`${dot} ${s.name}.service - ${s.desc}`, ` Loaded: loaded (/usr/lib/systemd/system/${s.name}.service; ${s.enabled ? 'enabled' : 'disabled'}; preset: enabled)`, ` Active: ${s.active ? C.style(['bold', 'green'], 'active (running)') : 'inactive (dead)'}${s.active ? ' since ' + since : ''}`]; if (s.active) { lines.push(` Docs: man:${s.name}(8)`, ` Main PID: ${s.pid} (${s.name})`, ` Tasks: ${TX.randInt(1, 40)} (limit: 18898)`, ` Memory: ${(Math.random() * 40 + 2).toFixed(1)}M (peak: ${(Math.random() * 60 + 5).toFixed(1)}M)`, ` CPU: ${TX.randInt(20, 3000)}ms`, ` CGroup: /system.slice/${s.name}.service`, ` └─${s.pid} ${c.m.procs.find((p) => p.pid === s.pid) ? c.m.procs.find((p) => p.pid === s.pid).cmd : '/usr/sbin/' + s.name}`); lines.push('', `${TX.MON[d.getMonth()]} ${d.getDate()} ${TX.p2(d.getHours())}:${TX.p2(d.getMinutes())}:02 ${c.m.net.hostname} systemd[1]: Starting ${s.name}.service - ${s.desc}...`, `${TX.MON[d.getMonth()]} ${d.getDate()} ${TX.p2(d.getHours())}:${TX.p2(d.getMinutes())}:03 ${c.m.net.hostname} systemd[1]: Started ${s.name}.service - ${s.desc}.`); } return lines.join('\n'); } def('systemctl', (c) => { const [sub, ...rest] = c.args.filter((a) => !a.startsWith('--')); const name = (rest[0] || '').replace(/\.service$/, ''); const svc = c.m.services.find((s) => s.name === name); switch (sub || 'list-units') { case 'status': if (!name) { c.outln(`${C.style(['bold', 'green'], '●')} ${c.m.net.hostname}\n State: running\n Units: 318 loaded (incl. loaded aliases)\n Jobs: 0 queued\n Failed: 0 units\n Since: ${TX.isoDate(c.m.boot)} EDT; ${TX.relTime(c.m.boot)}`); return; } if (!svc) { c.err(`Unit ${name}.service could not be found.\n`); return 4; } c.outln(svcStatus(c, svc)); return svc.active ? 0 : 3; case 'start': case 'stop': case 'restart': case 'enable': case 'disable': case 'reload': { if (!svc) { c.err(`Failed to ${sub} ${name}.service: Unit ${name}.service not found.\n`); return 5; } if (c.user !== 'root') { c.err(`${C.style(['bold', 'red'], '==== AUTHENTICATING FOR org.freedesktop.systemd1.manage-units ====')}\nAuthentication is required to ${sub} '${name}.service'.\nAuthenticating as: Student,,, (student)\n${C.dim('(tip: use sudo systemctl ' + sub + ' ' + name + ')')}\n`); c.err(`Failed to ${sub} ${name}.service: Access denied\n`); return 1; } if (sub === 'start' || sub === 'restart') { if (!svc.active || sub === 'restart') { if (svc.pid) c.m.kill(svc.pid); svc.pid = c.m.spawn(name, { user: 'root', cmd: '/usr/sbin/' + name, tty: '?' }).pid; } svc.active = true; } if (sub === 'stop') { svc.active = false; if (svc.pid) c.m.kill(svc.pid); svc.pid = null; } if (sub === 'enable') { svc.enabled = true; c.err(`Synchronizing state of ${name}.service with SysV service script with /usr/lib/systemd/systemd-sysv-install.\nExecuting: /usr/lib/systemd/systemd-sysv-install enable ${name}\n`); } if (sub === 'disable') { svc.enabled = false; c.err(`Removed "/etc/systemd/system/multi-user.target.wants/${name}.service".\n`); } return 0; } case 'is-active': if (!svc) { c.outln('inactive'); return 3; } c.outln(svc.active ? 'active' : 'inactive'); return svc.active ? 0 : 3; case 'is-enabled': if (!svc) return 1; c.outln(svc.enabled ? 'enabled' : 'disabled'); return 0; case 'list-units': case 'list-unit-files': { const rows = [['UNIT', 'LOAD', 'ACTIVE', 'SUB', 'DESCRIPTION']].concat(c.m.services.filter((s) => s.active || c.args.includes('--all')).map((s) => [s.name + '.service', 'loaded', s.active ? 'active' : 'inactive', s.active ? 'running' : 'dead', s.desc])); c.outln(TX.table(rows, [], ' ') + `\n\nLegend: LOAD → Reflects whether the unit definition was properly loaded.\n ACTIVE → The high-level unit activation state, i.e. generalization of SUB.\n SUB → The low-level unit activation state, values depend on unit type.\n\n${rows.length - 1} loaded units listed.`); return 0; } case 'reboot': case 'poweroff': return cmdsShutdown(c); default: c.err(`Unknown command verb '${sub}'.\n`); return 1; } }, { only: LX }); def('service', (c) => { const [name, action] = c.args; if (c.args[0] === '--status-all') { c.m.services.forEach((s) => c.outln(` [ ${s.active ? C.green(s) : s), cb = (s) => (color && c.tty ? C.blue(s) : s), cm = (s) => (color && c.tty ? C.magenta(s) : s); if (!obj) { c.err('Usage: ip [ OPTIONS ] OBJECT { COMMAND | help }\nwhere OBJECT := { address | link | route | neigh | ... }\n'); return 255; } if ('address'.startsWith(obj) || obj === 'a' || obj === 'addr') { if (brief) { c.outln(`lo UNKNOWN 127.0.0.1/8 ::1/128\n${n.iface.padEnd(16)} UP ${n.ip}/24 ${n.ip6}/64\n${n.wifi.padEnd(16)} DOWN `); return; } void sub; c.err(`Object "${obj}" is unknown, try "ip help".\n`); return 1; }, { only: LX }); def('ifconfig', (c) => { const want = c.args.find((a) => !a.startsWith('-')); if (want) { let buf = ''; const sub = Object.assign({}, c, { outln: (s) => { buf += s + '\n'; }, out: (s) => { buf += s; } }); TX.posixCmds.ifconfig.fn(Object.assign(sub, { args: [] })); const blocks = buf.split(/\n(?=\S)/); const hit = blocks.find((b) => b.startsWith(want + ':')); }, { only: UNIX, pkgLinux: 'net-tools' }); def('ping', async (c) => { const { f, rest } = getopt(c.args, 'c:i:W:w:s:t:qn46'); const host = rest[0]; const mac = c.m.kind === 'mac'; if (!host) { c.err(mac ? 'usage: ping [-AaDdfnoQqRrv] [-c count] [-G sweepmaxsize] host\n' : 'ping: usage error: Destination address required\n'); return mac ? 64 : 1; } const ip = TX.resolve(host); if (!ip) { c.err(mac ? `ping: cannot resolve ${host}: Unknown host\n` : `ping: ${host}: ${/\./.test(host) ? 'Name or service not known' : 'Temporary failure in name resolution'}\n`); return 2; } const count = f.c ? parseInt(f.c, 10) : Infinity; const size = parseInt(f.s || 56, 10); const ttl = ip.startsWith('192.168') || ip.startsWith('127') ? 64 : ip.startsWith('142') ? 117 : 54; const rtts = []; const t0 = Date.now(); c.outln(mac ? `PING ${host} (${ip}): ${size} data bytes` : `PING ${host} (${ip}) ${size}(${size + 28}) bytes of data.`); let seq = mac ? 0 : 1, sent = 0; const rname = host === ip ? ip : (/google/.test(host) ? `lga34s32-in-f14.1e100.net (${ip})` : `${host} (${ip})`); try { while (sent < count) { const rtt = TX.latency(host) * (0.9 + Math.random() * 0.25); sent++; await c.sleep(Math.max(rtt, 30)); const lost = Math.random() < 0.02 && ip.startsWith('9'); if (!lost) { rtts.push(rtt); if (!f.q) c.outln(mac ? `${size + 8} bytes from ${ip}: icmp_seq=${seq} ttl=${ttl} time=${rtt.toFixed(3)} ms` : `${size + 8} bytes from ${rname}: icmp_seq=${seq} ttl=${ttl} time=${rtt < 10 ? rtt.toFixed(2) : rtt.toFixed(1)} ms`); } seq++; if (sent < count) await c.sleep(Math.max(100, parseFloat(f.i || 1) * 1000 - rtt)); } } catch (e) { if (!e.interrupt) throw e; c.out('\n'); } const loss = sent ? Math.round(((sent - rtts.length) / sent) * 100) : 0; const mn = Math.min(...rtts), mx = Math.max(...rtts), avg = rtts.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / (rtts.length || 1); const mdev = Math.sqrt(rtts.reduce((a, b) => a + (b - avg) ** 2, 0) / (rtts.length || 1)); c.outln(`\n--- ${host} ping statistics ---\n${sent} packets transmitted, ${rtts.length} ${mac ? 'packets received' : 'received'}, ${mac ? loss.toFixed(1) : loss}% packet loss${mac ? '' : `, time ${Date.now() - t0}ms`}`); if (rtts.length) c.outln(`${mac ? 'round-trip' : 'rtt'} min/avg/max/${mac ? 'stddev' : 'mdev'} = ${mn.toFixed(3)}/${avg.toFixed(3)}/${mx.toFixed(3)}/${mdev.toFixed(3)} ms`); return rtts.length ? 0 : 1; }); def('traceroute tracepath', async (c) => { const host = c.args.filter((a) => !a.startsWith('-'))[0]; if (!host) return c.fail('missing host operand'); const hops = TX.hops(host); if (!hops) { c.err(`${host}: Name or service not known\nCannot handle "host" cmdline arg \`${host}' on position 1 (argc 1)\n`); return 2; } c.outln(`traceroute to ${host} (${TX.resolve(host)}), ${c.m.kind === 'mac' ? '64 hops max, 40 byte packets' : '30 hops max, 60 byte packets'}`); try { for (let i = 0; i < hops.length; i++) { const [h, ip, ms] = hops[i]; await c.sleep(250 + ms * 8); if (h === '*') { c.outln(`${String(i + 1).padStart(2)} * * *`); continue; } const t = [ms, ms * (0.95 + Math.random() * 0.1), ms * (0.95 + Math.random() * 0.12)].map((x) => x.toFixed(3) + ' ms'); c.outln(`${String(i + 1).padStart(2)} ${h} (${ip}) ${t.join(' ')}`); } } catch (e) { if (!e.interrupt) throw e; c.out('\n'); return 130; } }, { pkgLinux: 'traceroute' }); def('dig', (c) => { const args = c.args.filter((a) => !a.startsWith('@')); const short = args.includes('+short'); const host = args.filter((a) => !a.startsWith('+') && !a.startsWith('-') && !['A', 'AAAA', 'MX', 'NS', 'TXT', 'CNAME', 'ANY'].includes(a.toUpperCase()))[0] || '.'; const type = (args.find((a) => ['A', 'AAAA', 'MX', 'NS', 'TXT', 'CNAME', 'ANY'].includes(a.toUpperCase())) || 'A').toUpperCase(); const recs = TX.DNS[host.toLowerCase()]; let answers = []; if (recs) { if (type === 'A') answers = recs.filter((x) => x.includes('.')).map((x) => [host + '.', TX.randInt(60, 300), 'IN', 'A', x]); if (type === 'AAAA') answers = recs.filter((x) => x.includes(':')).map((x) => [host + '.', TX.randInt(60, 300), 'IN', 'AAAA', x]); if (type === 'MX') answers = [[host + '.', 300, 'IN', 'MX', '10 mail.' + host + '.']]; if (type === 'NS') answers = [1, 2].map((k) => [host + '.', 86400, 'IN', 'NS', `ns${k}.${host}.`]); if (type === 'TXT') answers = [[host + '.', 3600, 'IN', 'TXT', '"v=spf1 include:_spf.' + host + ' ~all"']]; } if (short) { answers.forEach((a) => c.outln(a[4])); return; } const server = c.m.kind === 'mac' ? '192.168.1.1' : '127.0.0.53'; c.outln(`\n; <<>> DiG 9.18.28-0ubuntu0.24.04.1-Ubuntu <<>> ${c.args.join(' ')}\n;; global options: +cmd\n;; Got answer:\n;; ->>HEADER<<- opcode: QUERY, status: ${recs ? 'NOERROR' : 'NXDOMAIN'}, id: ${TX.randInt(1000, 65000)}\n;; flags: qr rd ra; QUERY: 1, ANSWER: ${answers.length}, AUTHORITY: 0, ADDITIONAL: 1\n\n;; OPT PSEUDOSECTION:\n; EDNS: version: 0, flags:; udp: 65494\n;; QUESTION SECTION:\n;${host}.\t\t\tIN\t${type}\n` + (answers.length ? `\n;; ANSWER SECTION:\n${answers.map((a) => a.join('\t')).join('\n')}\n` : '') + `\n;; Query time: ${TX.randInt(2, 40)} msec\n;; SERVER: ${server}#53(${server}) (UDP)\n;; WHEN: ${TX.unixDate(Date.now())}\n;; MSG SIZE rcvd: ${TX.randInt(40, 120)}\n`); }, { only: ['linux', 'mac'] }); def('nslookup', (c) => { const host = c.args.filter((a) => !a.startsWith('-'))[0]; if (!host) { c.outln('> (interactive mode is not supported in TerminalEX; try: nslookup google.com)'); return; } const recs = TX.DNS[host.toLowerCase()]; const server = c.sh.flavor === 'gitbash' ? null : c.m.kind === 'mac' ? [URL]...\n'); return 1; } const full = /^https?:/.test(url) ? url : 'http://' + url; const host = full.replace(/^https?:\/\//, '').split('/')[0]; const ip = TX.resolve(host); if (!f.q) c.err(`--${TX.isoDate(Date.now())}-- ${full}\nResolving ${host} (${host})... `); }, { only: LX }); // ------------------------------------------------------------------ packages async function aptInstall(c, names, remove) { const sh = c.sh; if (c.user !== 'root') { c.err(`E: Could not open lock file /var/lib/dpkg/lock-frontend - open (13: Permission denied)\nE: Unable to acquire the dpkg frontend lock (/var/lib/dpkg/lock-frontend), are you root?\n`); Done\nBuilding dependency tree... Done\nReading state information... Done'); const todo = []; for (const n of names) { const p = TX.pkgName(n); if (!p) { c.err(`E: Unable to locate package ${n}\n`); return 100; } if (!remove && c.m.installed.has(p)) { c.outln(`${n} is already the newest version (${TX.PKGS[p].ver}).`); continue; } if (remove && !c.m.installed.has(p)) { c.outln(`Package '${n}' is not installed, so not removed`); continue; } todo.push(p); } if (!todo.length) { c.outln(`0 upgraded, 0 newly installed, 0 to remove and 12 not upgraded.`); return 0; } const kb = todo.reduce((s, p) => s + TX.PKGS[p].kb, 0); if (remove) { c.outln(`The following packages will be REMOVED:\n ${todo.join(' ')}\n0 upgraded, 0 newly installed, ${todo.length} to remove and 12 not upgraded.\nAfter this operation, ${(kb * 4.2).toFixed(1)} kB disk space will be freed.`); const ans = c.args.includes('-y') ? 'y' : await c.readLine('Do you want to continue? [Y/n] '); if (/^n/i.test(ans)) { c.outln('Abort.'); return 1; } c.outln('(Reading database ... 203512 files and directories currently installed.)'); for (const p of todo) { c.outln(`Removing ${p} (${TX.PKGS[p].ver}) ...`); c.m.installed.delete(p); await c.sleep(250); } c.outln('Processing triggers for man-db (2.12.0-4build2) ...'); TX.app.save(); return 0; } c.outln(`The following NEW packages will be installed:\n ${todo.join(' ')}\n0 upgraded, ${todo.length} newly installed, 0 to remove and 12 not upgraded.\nNeed to get ${kb.toFixed(1)} kB of archives.\nAfter this operation, ${(kb * 4.2).toFixed(1)} kB of additional disk space will be used.`); if (todo.length > 1 || kb > 500) { const ans = c.args.includes('-y') ? 'y' : await c.readLine('Do you want to continue? [Y/n] '); if (/^n/i.test(ans)) { c.outln('Abort.'); return 1; } } let k = 1; for (const p of todo) { await c.sleep(300); c.outln(`Get:${k++} http://archive.ubuntu.com/ubuntu noble/universe amd64 ${p} amd64 ${TX.PKGS[p].ver} [${TX.PKGS[p].kb} kB]`); } c.outln(`Fetched ${kb.toFixed(1)} kB in 1s (${TX.randInt(200, 900)} kB/s)`); for (const p of todo) { c.outln(`Selecting previously unselected package ${p}.\n(Reading database ... 203512 files and directories currently installed.)\nPreparing to unpack .../${p}_${TX.PKGS[p].ver}_amd64.deb ...`); for (let pct = 0; pct <= 100; pct += 25) { c.out(`\r\x1b[42m\x1b[30mProgress: [${String(pct).padStart(3)}%]\x1b[0m [${'#'.repeat(pct / 2.5).padEnd(40, '.')}] `); await c.sleep(120); } c.out('\r\x1b[2K'); c.outln(`Unpacking ${p} (${TX.PKGS[p].ver}) ...`); c.outln(`Setting up ${p} (${TX.PKGS[p].ver}) ...`); c.m.installed.add(p); } c.outln('Processing triggers for man-db (2.12.0-4build2) ...'); TX.app.save(); void sh; return 0; } def('apt apt-get', async (c) => { const args = c.args.filter((a) => !a.startsWith('-')); const sub = args[0]; switch (sub) { case 'update': { if (c.user !== 'root') { c.err('Reading package lists... Object.keys(v).length : 0); continue; } if (st === 'type') { next.push(v === null ? 'null' : Array.isArray(v) ? 'array' : typeof v); continue; } let cur = [v]; const re = /\.([A-Za-z_][\w]*)|\[(\d*)\]|\["([^"]+)"\]/g; let m, any = false; while ((m = re.exec(st))) { any = true; const nx = []; for (const x of cur) { if (m[1] !== undefined) nx.push(x == null ? null : x[m[1]] === undefined ? null : x[m[1]]); else if (m[3] !== undefined) nx.push(x == null ? null : x[m[3]] ?? null); else if (m[2] === '') { if (Array.isArray(x)) nx.push(...x); else if (x && typeof x === 'object') nx.push(...Object.values(x)); else throw new Error(`Cannot iterate over ${x === null ? 'null' : typeof x}`); } else nx.push(Array.isArray(x) ? x[+m[2]] ?? null : null); } cur = nx; } if (!any) throw new Error(`${st}/0 is not defined`); next.push(...cur); } vals = next; } return vals; }; // ------------------------------------------------------------------ git TX.gitCtx = (base) => Object.assign({ editor: (segs) => (/vi/.test(base.editorName || '') ? override() only changes them on the listed kinds. */ (function () { 'use strict'; const TX = window.TX; const C = TX.C; const cmds = TX.posixCmds; const def = TX.defPosix; const getopt = (...a) => TX.getopt(...a); const SOL = ['solaris'], BSD = ['freebsd'], V7 = ['unix'], NEWUNIX = ['solaris', 'freebsd'], ALLNEW = ['solaris', 'freebsd', 'unix']; // Make an existing command available on more kinds of machine (keeping its output). function addKinds(name, kinds) { const c = cmds[name]; if (c && c.only) kinds.forEach((k) => { if (!c.only.includes(k)) c.only = c.only.concat(k); }); } // Replace a command's behavior on some kinds only; everywhere else the original runs. function override(name, kinds, fn, extra) { const orig = cmds[name]; const o = Object.assign({}, orig || {}, extra || {}); if (orig && orig.only) o.only = orig.only.concat(kinds.filter((k) => !orig.only.includes(k))); else if (!orig) o.only = kinds.slice(); o.fn = (c) => (kinds.includes(c.m.kind) ? fn(c, orig) : orig.fn(c)); const hms = (d) => hm(d) + ':' + TX.p2(d.getSeconds()); const human = (b) => { if (b >= 1024 ** 4) return (b / 1024 ** 4).toFixed(2) + 'T'; if (b >= 1024 ** 3) return (b / 1024 ** 3 >= 100 ? Math.round(b / 1024 ** 3) : (b / 1024 ** 3).toFixed(1)) + 'G'; if (b >= 1024 ** 2) return (b / 1024 ** 2 >= 100 ? Math.round(b / 1024 ** 2) : (b / 1024 ** 2).toFixed(1)) + 'M'; return Math.max(1, Math.round(b / 1024)) + 'K'; }; const table = (rows, align) => TX.table(rows.map((r) => r.map(String)), align); ['who', 'mount', 'top', 'ifconfig', 'netstat', 'su', 'last', 'crontab'].forEach((n) => addKinds(n, NEWUNIX)); addKinds('who', V7); addKinds('su', V7); addKinds('mount', V7); addKinds('sudo', NEWUNIX); // Solaris 11 ships sudo; FreeBSD only after pkg install sudo (pkgOn below) cmds.sudo.pkgOn = { freebsd: 'sudo' }; // Shells you can start by name on the new UNIXes (FreeBSD and V7: sh; Solaris: sh, ksh and bash). addKinds('sh', ALLNEW); addKinds('bash', SOL); cmds.ksh = Object.assign({}, cmds.sh, { only: SOL.concat(['mac']) }); def('fc', (c) => { // fc -l: list recent commands (the POSIX/ksh way to see history) const H = c.sh.history || []; if (c.args[0] === '-l' || !c.args.length) { H.slice(-16).forEach((h, i, a) => c.outln(String(H.length - a.length + i + 1).padEnd(6) + h)); return 0; } c.err('fc: only fc -l is simulated in TerminalEX\n'); return 1; }, { only: ['linux', 'mac', 'gitbash', 'solaris', 'freebsd'] }); // ------------------------------------------------------------------ uname override('uname', NEWUNIX, (c) => { const k = c.m.kind, n = c.m.net.hostname; const f = { solaris: { s: 'SunOS', n, r: '5.11', v: '11.4.72.176.1', m: 'sun4v', p: 'sparc', i: 'sun4v' }, freebsd: { s: 'FreeBSD', n, r: '14.1-RELEASE', v: 'FreeBSD 14.1-RELEASE releng/14.1-n267679-10e31f0946d8 GENERIC', m: 'amd64', p: 'amd64', i: 'GENERIC', K: '1401000', U: '1401000' }, }[k]; const a = c.args.join('').replace(/-/g, ''); if (!a) { c.outln(f.s); return; } if (a.includes('a')) { c.outln(k === 'solaris' ? c.args[ti + 1] : 'filesystem'; const name = c.args.slice(1).filter((a, i, arr) => !a.startsWith('-') && arr[i - 1] !== '-t' && arr[i - 1] !== '-o')[0]; let rows = []; if (type !== 'snapshot') rows = rows.concat(Z.map((d) => [d.name, human(d.used), human(d.avail), human(d.refer), d.mount])); if (type === 'snapshot' || type === 'all') rows = rows.concat(X.snaps.map((s) => [s.name, human(s.used), '-', human(s.refer), '-'])); if (name) { rows = rows.filter((r) => r[0] === name || r[0].startsWith(name + '/') || r[0].startsWith(name + '@')); if (!rows.length) return c.fail(`cannot open '${name}': dataset does not exist`); } if (!rows.length) { c.outln('no datasets available'); return; } c.outln(table([['NAME', 'USED', 'AVAIL', 'REFER', 'MOUNTPOINT']].concat(rows), ['l', 'r', 'r', 'r', 'l'])); return; } if (sub === 'create') { const name = c.args.filter((a) => !a.startsWith('-'))[1]; if (!name) { c.err('missing filesystem argument\n'); return 2; } if (need(`create '${name}'`)) return 1; if (Z.some((d) => d.name === name)) return c.fail(`cannot create '${name}': dataset already exists`); const parent = Z.find((d) => d.name === name.split('/').slice(0, -1).join('/')); if (!parent) return c.fail(`cannot create '${name}': parent does not exist`); const mount = parent.mount.startsWith('/') ? c.stdin.split('\n') : null; let li = 0; const read = async (p) => { if (lines) return li < lines.length ? lines[li++] : null; try { return await c.readLine(p); } catch (e) { return null; } }; if (!lines && c.tty) c.err(C.dim('(ed: no screen, no cursor — you type commands. a = append text, . alone ends it, p/n print, w saves, q quits, h explains a ?)') + '\n'); const addr = (s) => { // returns [rest, [a1, a2] | null] let m; const one = (t) => { if ((m = /^(\d+)/.exec(t))) return [t.slice(m[1].length), parseInt(m[1], 10)]; if (t[0] === '.') return [t.slice(1), dot]; if (t[0] === '$') return [t.slice(1), buf.length]; if ((m = /^\/([^/]*)\/?/.exec(t))) { const re = new RegExp(m[1]); for (let k = 1; k <= buf.length; k++) { const i = ((dot + k - 1) % buf.length) + 1; if (re.test(buf[i - 1])) return [t.slice(m[0].length), i]; } return [t.slice(m[0].length), -1]; } if ((m = /^([+-])(\d*)/.exec(t)) && m[0]) return [t.slice(m[0].length), dot + (m[1] === '+' ? 1 : -1) * (m[2] ? parseInt(m[2], 10) : 1)]; return [t, null]; }; if (s[0] === ',' || s[0] === '%') return [s.slice(1), [1, buf.length]]; let [r, a] = one(s); if (a === null) return [s, null]; if (r[0] === ',') { const [r2, b] = one(r.slice(1)); return [r2, [a, b === null ? a : b]]; } return [r, [a, a]]; }; for (;;) { const line = await read(''); if (line === null) break; let [rest, rng] = addr(line); const cmd = rest[0] || ''; const arg = rest.slice(1).trim(); if (rng && (rng[0] < 0 || rng[1] < 0 || rng[0] > rng[1] || rng[1] > buf.length || (rng[0] === 0 && !'aqr'.includes(cmd)))) { fail('invalid address'); continue; } const [a1, a2] = rng || [dot, dot]; const input = async () => { const out = []; for (;;) { const l = await read(''); if (l === null || l === '.') break; out.push(l); } return out; }; if (cmd === '' && rng) { dot = a2; c.outln(buf[dot - 1]); continue; } if (cmd === '') { if (dot < buf.length) { dot++; c.outln(buf[dot - 1]); } else fail('invalid address'); continue; } if (cmd === 'a' || cmd === 'i') { const t = await input(); const at = cmd === 'a' ? a2 : Math.max(0, a1 - 1); buf.splice(at, 0, ...t); dot = at + t.length; dirty = dirty || t.length > 0; continue; } if (cmd === 'c') { const t = await input(); buf.splice(a1 - 1, a2 - a1 + 1, ...t); dot = a1 - 1 + t.length; dirty = true; continue; } if (cmd === 'd') { if (!buf.length) { fail('invalid address'); continue; } buf.splice(a1 - 1, a2 - a1 + 1); dot = Math.min(a1, buf.length); dirty = true; continue; } if (cmd === 'p' || cmd === 'n' || cmd === 'l') { if (!buf.length) { fail('invalid address'); continue; } for (let k = a1; k <= a2; k++) c.outln((cmd === 'n' ? k + '\t' : '') + buf[k - 1] + (cmd === 'l' ? '$' : '')); dot = a2; continue; } if (cmd === 's') { const m = /^(.)(.*?)\1(.*?)(?:\1(g?))?$/.exec(rest.slice(1)); if (!m) { fail('no match'); continue; } const re = new RegExp(m[2], m[4] ? 'g' : ''); let hit = false; for (let k = a1; k <= a2; k++) if (re.test(buf[k - 1])) { re.lastIndex = 0; buf[k - 1] = buf[k - 1].replace(re, m[3].replace(/&/g, '$&')); hit = true; dot = k; } if (!hit) fail('no match'); else dirty = true; continue; } if (cmd === 'g' && rest[1] === '/') { const m = /^g\/([^/]*)\/(.*)$/.exec(rest); if (!m) { fail('unknown command'); continue; } const re = new RegExp(m[1]); const sub = m[2] || 'p'; for (let k = (rng ? a1 : 1); k <= (rng ? a2 : buf.length); k++) if (re.test(buf[k - 1])) { if (sub === 'p') c.outln(buf[k - 1]); else if (sub === 'n') c.outln(k + '\t' + buf[k - 1]); dot = k; } continue; } if (cmd === '=') { c.outln(String(rng ? a2 : buf.length)); continue; } if (cmd === 'w' || cmd === 'W') { const name = arg || fname; if (!name) { fail('no current filename'); continue; } const text = buf.length ? buf.join('\n') + '\n' : ''; try { c.vfs.writeFile(c.path(name), text, { user: c.user, owner: c.user }); } catch (e) { fail('cannot open output file'); continue; } fname = name; dirty = false; if (!quiet) c.outln(String(text.length)); if (rest === 'wq' || rest.startsWith('wq ')) break; continue; } if (cmd === 'f') { if (arg) fname = arg; c.outln(fname || '?'); continue; } if (cmd === 'r') { try { const t = c.vfs.readFile(c.path(arg || fname), { user: c.user }).split('\n'); if (t[t.length - 1] === '') t.pop(); buf.splice(a2, 0, ...t); dot = a2 + t.length; dirty = true; if (!quiet) c.outln(String(t.join('\n').length + 1)); } catch (e) { fail('cannot open input file'); } continue; } if (cmd === 'h' || cmd === 'H') { if (cmd === 'H') help = !help; if (lastErr) c.outln(lastErr); continue; } if (cmd === 'q') { if (dirty && rest !== 'Q') { dirty = false; fail('warning: buffer modified (type q again to quit without saving, or w to save)'); continue; } break; } if (cmd === 'Q') break; fail('unknown command'); } }); })();
